{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01CfB2iuwyT6PfrPxY3mgqEe","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01SfepTodCvNX25JJqf8u4fV","name":"mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_execute","input":{"server":"westb-48145","command":"cat > /root/entrainment_gated_ft/results/exp005_zoo_expansion/run_pure_v7.sh << 'SCRIPT'\n#!/bin/bash\nset -eo pipefail\n\nsource /etc/network_turbo\nsource /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base\nexport HF_HOME=/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\nexport HF_HUB_DISABLE_XET=1\nexport HF_ENDPOINT=https://hf-mirror.com\nexport REQUESTS_CA_BUNDLE=/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt\nexport SSL_CERT_FILE=/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt\n\nBASEDIR=\"/root/entrainment_gated_ft\"\nOUTBASE=\"$BASEDIR/results/exp005_zoo_expansion\"\n\ndeclare -A MODELS\nMODELS[\"GLA\"]=\"$BASEDIR/models/340M-20B-GLA-pure-baseline\"\nMODELS[\"DeltaNet\"]=\"$BASEDIR/models/340M-20B-DeltaNet-pure\"\nMODELS[\"HGRN\"]=\"$BASEDIR/models/340M-20B-HGRN-pure-baseline\"\n\nfor NAME in GLA DeltaNet HGRN; do\n    LOCAL=\"${MODELS[$NAME]}\"\n    OUTDIR=\"$OUTBASE/${NAME}_pure_baseline\"\n\n    if [ -d \"$OUTDIR\" ] && ls \"$OUTDIR\"/results_*.json 1>/dev/null 2>&1; then\n        echo \"[SKIP] $NAME already evaluated at $OUTDIR\"\n        continue\n    fi\n\n    echo \"==========================================\"\n    echo \"[EVAL] $NAME from $LOCAL\"\n    echo \"==========================================\"\n\n    mkdir -p \"$OUTDIR\"\n\n    python3 -c \"\nimport fla\nimport sys\nsys.argv = [\n    'lm_eval',\n    '--model', 'hf',\n    '--model_args', 'pretrained=${LOCAL},trust_remote_code=True',\n    '--tasks', 'lambada_openai,arc_easy',\n    '--device', 'cuda:0',\n    '--batch_size', 'auto',\n    '--log_samples',\n    '--output_path', '${OUTDIR}/',\n]\nfrom lm_eval.__main__ import cli_evaluate\ncli_evaluate()\n\"\n\n    echo \"[DONE] $NAME eval complete. Results at $OUTDIR\"\ndone\n\necho \"==========================================\"\necho \"ALL PURE BASELINE EVALS COMPLETE\"\necho \"==========================================\"\nSCRIPT\nchmod +x /root/entrainment_gated_ft/results/exp005_zoo_expansion/run_pure_v7.sh"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":241,"cache_read_input_tokens":37380,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":241},"output_tokens":69,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"f91c7e3f-c021-4ec0-8958-c2b588a62e76","uuid":"af53ca43-40c5-4e0e-bd49-fbb4367a6408"}

do\n    LOCAL=\"${MODELS[$NAME]}\"\n    OUTDIR=\"$OUTBASE/${NAME}_pure_baseline\"\n\n    if [ -d \"$OUTDIR\" ] && ls \"$OUTDIR\"/results_*.json 1>/dev/null 2>&1; then\n        echo \"[SKIP] $NAME already evaluated at $OUTDIR\"\n        continue\n    fi\n\n    echo \"==========================================\"\n    echo \"[EVAL] $NAME from $LOCAL\"\n    echo \"==========================================\"\n\n    mkdir -p \"$OUTDIR\"\n\n    python3 << PYEOF\nimport fla\n# Monkey-patch: transformers 5.x expects _tied_weights_keys as dict, fla provides list\nimport fla.models.gla.modeling_gla as _gla\nimport fla.models.delta_net.modeling_delta_net as _dn\nimport fla.models.hgrn.modeling_hgrn as _hgrn\nimport fla.models.gated_deltanet.modeling_gated_deltanet as _gdn\n\nfor mod in [_gla, _dn, _hgrn, _gdn]:\n    for attr_name in dir(mod):\n        obj = getattr(mod, attr_name, None)\n        if isinstance(obj, type) and hasattr(obj, '_tied_weights_keys'):\n            if isinstance(obj._tied_weights_keys, list):\n                obj._tied_weights_keys = {}\n\nimport sys\nsys.argv = [\n    'lm_eval',\n    '--model', 'hf',\n    '--model_args', 'pretrained=${LOCAL},trust_remote_code=True',\n    '--tasks', 'lambada_openai,arc_easy',\n    '--device', 'cuda:0',\n    '--batch_size', 'auto',\n    '--log_samples',\n    '--output_path', '${OUTDIR}/',\n]\nfrom lm_eval.__main__ import cli_evaluate\ncli_evaluate()\nPYEOF\n\n    echo \"[DONE] $NAME eval complete. Results at $OUTDIR\"\ndone\n\necho \"==========================================\"\necho \"ALL PURE BASELINE EVALS COMPLETE\"\necho \"==========================================\"\nSCRIPT\nchmod +x /root/entrainment_gated_ft/results/exp005_zoo_expansion/run_pure_v8.sh"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1844,"cache_read_input_tokens":43214,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1844},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"f91c7e3f-c021-4ec0-8958-c2b588a62e76","uuid":"516641e8-53ab-4a67-8092-d2bf716d4c7c"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01RVa3VWsKEsr4GRWEraDfb1","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_0162txHxa9rjhU3aeLDHQrUk","name":"mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_execute","input":{"server":"westb-48145","command":"cat > /root/entrainment_gated_ft/results/exp005_zoo_expansion/run_pure_v9.sh << 'SCRIPT'\n#!/bin/bash\nset -eo pipefail\n\nsource /etc/network_turbo\nsource /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base\nexport HF_HOME=/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\nexport HF_HUB_DISABLE_XET=1\nexport HF_ENDPOINT=https://hf-mirror.com\nexport REQUESTS_CA_BUNDLE=/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt\nexport SSL_CERT_FILE=/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt\n\nBASEDIR=\"/root/entrainment_gated_ft\"\nOUTBASE=\"$BASEDIR/results/exp005_zoo_expansion\"\n\n# Step 1: Re-download HGRN from ModelScope (background)\necho \"[DOWNLOAD] Re-downloading HGRN from ModelScope...\"\npython3 -c \"\nfrom modelscope import snapshot_download\nmodel_dir = snapshot_download('m-a-p/340M-20B-HGRN-pure-baseline', cache_dir='/root/autodl-tmp/.ms_cache')\nprint('HGRN downloaded to:', model_dir)\n\nimport shutil, os\ndst = '/root/entrainment_gated_ft/models/340M-20B-HGRN-pure-baseline'\n# Copy model.safetensors from ModelScope cache\nsrc_st = os.path.join(model_dir, 'model.safetensors')\ndst_st = os.path.join(dst, 'model.safetensors')\nif not os.path.exists(dst_st) or os.path.getsize(dst_st) < 1_000_000_000:\n    shutil.copy2(src_st, dst_st)\n    print(f'Copied {src_st} -> {dst_st}')\n\" &\nHGRN_DL_PID=$!\necho \"[DOWNLOAD] HGRN download PID: $HGRN_DL_PID\"\n\n# Step 2: Eval GLA and DeltaNet (while HGRN downloads)\neval_model() {\n    local NAME=$1\n    local LOCAL=$2\n    local OUTDIR=\"$OUTBASE/${NAME}_pure_baseline\"\n\n    if [ -d \"$OUTDIR\" ] && ls \"$OUTDIR\"/results_*.json 1>/dev/null 2>&1; then\n        echo \"[SKIP] $NAME already evaluated at $OUTDIR\"\n        return\n    fi\n\n    echo \"==========================================\"\n    echo \"[EVAL] $NAME from $LOCAL\"\n    echo \"==========================================\"\n    mkdir -p \"$OUTDIR\"\n\n    python3 -c \"\nimport fla\n# Fix transformers 5.x compatibility: _tied_weights_keys must be dict not list\nimport fla.models.gla.modeling_gla as _gla\nimport fla.models.delta_net.modeling_delta_net as _dn\nimport fla.models.hgrn.modeling_hgrn as _hgrn\nimport fla.models.gated_deltanet.modeling_gated_deltanet as _gdn\n\nfor mod in [_gla, _dn, _hgrn, _gdn]:\n    for attr_name in dir(mod):\n        obj = getattr(mod, attr_name, None)\n        if isinstance(obj, type) and hasattr(obj, '_tied_weights_keys'):\n            if isinstance(obj._tied_weights_keys, list):\n                obj._tied_weights_keys = {'lm_head.weight': 'model.embeddings.weight'}\n\nimport sys\nsys.argv = [\n    'lm_eval',\n    '--model', 'hf',\n    '--model_args', 'pretrained=${LOCAL},trust_remote_code=True',\n    '--tasks', 'lambada_openai,arc_easy',\n    '--device', 'cuda:0',\n    '--batch_size', 'auto',\n    '--log_samples',\n    '--output_path', '${OUTDIR}/',\n]\nfrom lm_eval.__main__ import cli_evaluate\ncli_evaluate()\n\"\n    echo \"[DONE] $NAME eval complete. Results at $OUTDIR\"\n}\n\neval_model \"GLA\" \"$BASEDIR/models/340M-20B-GLA-pure-baseline\"\neval_model \"DeltaNet\" \"$BASEDIR/models/340M-20B-DeltaNet-pure\"\n\n# Step 3: Wait for HGRN download then eval\necho \"[WAIT] Waiting for HGRN download (PID $HGRN_DL_PID)...\"\nwait $HGRN_DL_PID\nHGRN_EXIT=$?\nif [ $HGRN_EXIT -ne 0 ]; then\n    echo \"[ERROR] HGRN download failed with exit code $HGRN_EXIT\"\n    exit 1\nfi\necho \"[DOWNLOAD] HGRN download complete\"\n\neval_model \"HGRN\" \"$BASEDIR/models/340M-20B-HGRN-pure-baseline\"\n\necho \"==========================================\"\necho \"ALL PURE BASELINE EVALS COMPLETE\"\necho \"==========================================\"\nSCRIPT\nchmod +x /root/entrainment_gated_ft/results/exp005_zoo_expansion/run_pure_v9.sh"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1082,"cache_read_input_tokens":64415,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1082},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"f91c7e3f-c021-4ec0-8958-c2b588a62e76","uuid":"4e356003-68f1-41a6-acdb-2e948aac7d72"}
